KanASu - 5555 Working Conditions
Understand how KAS works
============================================================

0001. [UI & Layout] Header must remain sticky on scroll at all viewport widths (variant 1-1)
0002. [UI & Layout] Logo must be visible and clickable on every page (variant 1-2)
0003. [UI & Layout] Navigation links must highlight the active section (variant 1-3)
0004. [UI & Layout] Primary CTA button must have sufficient contrast (variant 1-4)
0005. [UI & Layout] Hero headline must be readable at 320px width (variant 1-5)
0006. [UI & Layout] Feature grid must stack to single column on mobile (variant 1-6)
0007. [UI & Layout] Footer must not overlap content (variant 1-7)
0008. [UI & Layout] Dark background must maintain WCAG AA contrast for body text (variant 1-8)
0009. [UI & Layout] Orange accent must be used only for emphasis, never for large areas (variant 1-9)
0010. [UI & Layout] All interactive elements must have hover and focus states (variant 1-10)
